In order to adopt the layer-wise learning in LLM compression, we partition the whole model into multiple segments consisting of layers, thereby enabling more computationally efficient knowledge transfer for LLMs. Based on our theoretical analysis of cumulative error phenomenon, layer-wise curriculum learning accelerates convergence while stabilizing the knowledge transfer process. In addition, we present a feature caching method with a multi-threading strategy to efficiently address feature misalignment across layers, maximizing GPU utilization. Consequently, our method exhibits advanced model compression performance, as well as high computational efficiency in terms of minimized memory usage and short training hours. Experiments on multiple datasets show that the proposed method achieves state-of-the-art performance while reducing GPU memory usage and training hours by more than 50\% on BERT and GPT-2. Moreover, it outperforms the other pruning methods on LLaMA-family and Qwen models under the same training hours, with a lower GPU memory footprint.

</details>


### [203] [Block Parallelism For Efficient Distributed Long-Context Diffusion Language Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19242)
*Tarun Suresh,Pranshu Chaturvedi,Hangoo Kang,Parth Shroff,Ishan S. Khare,Hermann Kumbong,Azalia Mirhoseini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对块扩散语言模型（BDLM）长上下文训练中的通信与显存瓶颈，提出块并行（BP）及上下文分片的块并行（CSBP）两种新分布式并行维度，显著提升吞吐并保持训练语义，在多个模型与任务上取得大幅加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BDLM 在块间采用自回归依赖、块内并行去噪，但长上下文训练受限于分布式注意力通信与激活显存。现有上下文并行（CP）按位置切分“干净+加噪”拼接序列，需要同时通信共享的干净 K/V 以及块特定的加噪 K/V 及其梯度，开销大。

Method: 作者观察到 BDLM 目标函数在目标块上是可分离的，据此提出：(1) 块并行（BP），将每个加噪块的计算分配到单个 rank；(2) 为扩展到长上下文，进一步提出上下文分片的块并行（CSBP），在 BP 基础上把共享的干净序列也切分到各 rank。CSBP 使加噪 K/V 及其梯度保持本地、避免复制干净前缀，并保持 BDLM 训练语义不变。

Result: 在 16 张 H200 GPU、256K 上下文下，CSBP 相较最佳基线将监督微调（SFT）吞吐提升 1.18–1.45 倍，将自回归模型转 BDLM 的吞吐提升 1.27–1.33 倍，同时峰值 HBM 持平或更低；512K 时全模型加速达 1.61 倍。在 8 张 H100 GPU 上，CSBP 使 DFlash2 推测解码器训练在 512K 加速 2.48 倍、1M 加速 7.59 倍。在 12 小时 DiffusionGemma 26B-A4B SFT 对照实验中，CSBP 在每个训练检查点上于 SWE-bench Verified 与 Terminal-Bench Lite 均取得更高通过率。

Conclusion: CSBP 为 BDLM 长上下文训练提供了一种通信与显存高效、且保持训练语义的分布式并行方案，在吞吐、扩展性和下游任务质量上均优于现有上下文并行方法，并已开源代码。

Abstract: Block diffusion language models (BDLMs) combine autoregressive dependencies across blocks with parallel denoising within blocks, but long-context training is constrained by distributed attention communication and activation memory. Conventional context parallelism (CP) shards the combined clean-plus-corrupted sequence by position, communicating shared clean K/V together with block-specific corrupted K/V and their gradients. We observe that the BDLM objective separates over target blocks. We introduce block parallelism (BP), a new distributed parallelism dimension that assigns each corrupted-block computation to one rank. To scale BP to long contexts, we introduce context-sharded block parallelism (CSBP), which also shards the shared clean sequence across those ranks. CSBP keeps corrupted K/V and gradients local, avoids replicated clean prefixes, and preserves BDLM training semantics. On 16 H200 GPUs at 256K context, CSBP improves throughput over the best baseline by 1.18-1.45x for supervised fine-tuning and 1.27-1.33x for conversion of autoregressive models to BDLMs, while matching or reducing peak HBM. Full-model speedup reaches 1.61x at 512K. On eight H100 GPUs, CSBP accelerates DFlash2 speculative-decoder training by 2.48x at 512K and 7.59x at 1M. In matched 12-hour DiffusionGemma 26B-A4B SFT runs, CSBP achieves higher pass rates at every trained checkpoint on SWE-bench Verified and Terminal-Bench Lite. Code: https://github.com/ScalingIntelligence/Turbo-dLLM

</details>


### [204] [Randomized SVD Approximations for Spectral Co-Clustering of Word-Document Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19243)
*Fateme Mazdarani,Carlos Toxtli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种用于归一化谱联合聚类（spectral co-clustering）的随机化近似方法，以降低词-文档矩阵上SVD的计算开销；实验表明随机投影法在多数场景下更可靠，而基于采样的方法仅在较稠密矩阵上更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谱联合聚类是发现词-文档矩阵中潜在结构的有力工具，但其依赖的奇异值分解（SVD）在高维数据上计算代价高昂，需要寻求高效的近似方案以降低运行时间。

Method: 针对文档簇数与词簇数可能不同的二部文本数据，提出两种归一化谱联合聚类的随机化近似方法：第一种通过随机投影实现随机化SVD；第二种将部分SVD与逐元素随机采样相结合。实验在真实与合成数据集上与全SVD基线对比。

Result: 两种方法均能相对于全SVD基线降低运行时间，但其表现取决于矩阵稀疏性：随机投影法在所有测试设置下是更可靠的近似；基于采样的方法在较稠密矩阵上最有用，而在本已稀疏的文本数据上收益有限。

Conclusion: 随机化近似谱联合聚类方法应根据数据的内在结构（如稀疏性）来选择，不存在单一的最优方案。

Abstract: Spectral co-clustering is a useful tool for discovering latent structure in word-document matrices, but its reliance on singular value decomposition (SVD) can make standard formulations expensive on high-dimensional data. This paper presents two randomized approximations for normalized spectral co-clustering of bipartite text data when the numbers of document and word clusters may differ. The first method uses randomized SVD through random projection, while the second combines partial SVD with element-wise random sampling. Across real-world and synthetic datasets, both methods reduce runtime relative to the full-SVD baseline, but their behavior depends on matrix sparsity. The random projection method is the more reliable approximation across the tested settings, whereas the sampling-based method is most useful on denser matrices and provides limited benefit on already sparse text data. These results show that randomized approximations for spectral co-clustering should be selected according to the underlying structure of the data.

</details>


### [205] [Radio-Frequency Convolutional Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19279)
*Zhihui Gao,Shi-Yuan Ma,Yiran Chen,Dirk Englund,Tingjun Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出射频卷积神经网络（RF-CNN），将边缘设备无线收发器中原有的频率混频器复用为模拟卷积运算单元，在频域直接执行 CNN 推理，实现低至 0.72 fJ/MAC 的能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备（智能手机、可穿戴设备、无人机）算力不足以支撑现代神经网络，而额外添加边缘加速器会受限于尺寸、重量、功耗与成本（SWaP-C）。作者希望利用设备已有的无线通信硬件（频率混频器天然在时域做乘法、在频域执行卷积）来避免新增硬件，从而以极低开销实现 AI 推理。

Method: 将多通道卷积映射到不同频率音（frequency tones）上，由被动混频器单次通过即完成卷积运算；权重通过无线信号（over the air）传输，模拟硬件与通信链路共享，边缘设备仅需花费能量在数据准备与读出阶段。实验演示了该 RF-CNN 方案在无线信号分类、图像分类乃至可控图像生成等任务上的表现。

Result: RF-CNN 可运行最深 9 层、多达 2640 万参数的深度 CNN，性能接近全精度基线；能效低至 0.72 fJ/MAC（每次乘加运算），比在设备上额外添加的数字处理器低约两个数量级。

Conclusion: 该结果表明现有无线基础设施可被复用，从而为已有连接的数十亿设备带来高效、达到最新水平的 AI 推理能力，无需额外专用硬件。

Abstract: Running artificial intelligence (AI) models directly on edge devices such as smartphones, wearables, and drones offers low latency, pervasive scalability, and data privacy, but these devices rarely carry the computing capability that modern neural networks demand. Edge accelerators have been developed in response, yet each adds computing hardware to devices already constrained in size, weight, power, and cost (SWaP-C). An alternative lies in what these devices already carry: the frequency mixer in every wireless radio multiplies signals in time, natively performing convolution in the frequency domain. Here we introduce radio-frequency convolutional neural networks (RF-CNNs), which repurpose existing communication hardware for CNN inference. Multi-channel convolutions are mapped onto frequency tones for a passive mixer to execute in a single pass. We experimentally demonstrate that RF-CNN runs deep CNNs up to 26.4 million parameters and nine layers from classification of wireless signals and images to controllable image generation, close to full-precision performance. Because the weights arrive over the air and the analog hardware is shared with communication, the edge device spends energy only on data preparation and readout-down to 0.72 femtojoules per multiply-accumulate, two orders of magnitude less than it would cost on an added digital processor. These results suggest that deployed wireless infrastructure can bring efficient, state-of-the-art AI inference to the billions of devices it already connects.

</details>


### [206] [Learning-Induced Dynamical Transition in Recurrent Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19288)
*Varun Vaidya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种非平衡动力学平均场理论（DMFT），描述循环神经网络在学习过程中从混沌动力学向稳定任务相关行为的转变，并预测训练期间网络输出的时间演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习能够从根本上重塑循环神经网络的动力学，使其从初始的混沌活动转变为稳定的任务依赖行为，但这一学习驱动的动力学转变过程缺乏理论描述，需要建立相应框架来刻画其机制。

Method: 作者发展了一种非平衡动力学平均场理论（DMFT），将缓慢的反馈驱动学习过程建模为不断演化的有效反馈强度；通过推导学习全程的两时间关联函数，研究DMFT解的分岔，确定临界反馈强度及依赖学习率的临界时间，并刻画有效动力学景观随学习反馈结构的渐进形变。

Result: 学习过程驱动网络经历由DMFT解的分岔所定义的混沌到稳定动力学的转变；存在一个临界反馈强度，以及一个依赖于学习率的临界时间，将两种动力学状态分开，这一转变源于增长的已学习反馈结构对有效动力学景观的渐进形变；从未训练状态出发，理论能够预测训练期间网络输出的时间演化，并与数值模拟定量吻合。

Conclusion: 该非平衡DMFT框架有效刻画了循环神经网络在学习驱动下从混沌到稳定的动力学转变，并给出临界反馈强度与临界时间，理论预测与数值模拟定量一致，为理解学习中动力学重塑提供了理论工具。

Abstract: Learning in recurrent neural networks can fundamentally reshape their underlying dynamics, transforming initially chaotic activity into stable task-dependent behavior. We develop a non-equilibrium dynamical mean-field theory(DMFT) to describe this transition during learning. We show that a slow feedback-driven learning process generates an evolving effective feedback strength that drives the network through a transition from chaotic to stable dynamics defined by a bifurcation of the DMFT solution. By deriving the two-time correlation function throughout learning, we identify a critical feedback strength and a corresponding learning rate dependent critical time separating these regimes. The transition arises from the progressive deformation of an effective dynamical landscape by the growing learned feedback structure. Starting from the untrained state, the theory predicts the time evolution of the network output during training and shows quantitative agreement with numerical simulations.

</details>


### [207] [Personalized Federated Hierarchical Gaussian Processes for Privacy-Preserving Modeling of Heterogeneous Distributed Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19337)
*Xianjian Xie,Hao Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: pFedHGP 是一种个性化联邦分层高斯过程模型，通过将每个客户端的潜在函数分解为共享全局成分、共享全局核结构的客户端特定偏差以及灵活的局部残差，在数据不出本地的前提下结合稀疏诱导变量近似与联邦变分推断实现概率回归与分类，并输出完整预测分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际联邦学习场景中，数据分布在异构客户端上，既存在跨客户端共享的全局模式，也存在各客户端特有的个性化差异；同时需要保护数据隐私、降低通信开销，并为下游决策提供不确定性信息。现有方法难以同时兼顾全局共享、客户端个性化、概率预测与高效通信。

Method: 将每个客户端的潜在函数分解为三部分：共享全局成分、共享全局核结构的客户端特定偏差、以及灵活的局部残差；采用稀疏诱导变量（inducing variables）近似和联邦变分推断进行训练，原始数据保留在本地，服务器仅同步共享成分的低维统计量；并引入瞬时线性混合模型视角，将分层结构与多输出高斯过程建立联系。

Result: 在冲压吨位监测中，pFedHGP 仅使用 13.77% 的标记周期即实现完美的故障分类；在联邦空气质量建模中，无需集中各站点时间序列即可恢复地理区域划分。

Conclusion: pFedHGP 在异构联邦数据上有效融合全局共享与个性化建模，输出完整预测分布以支持不确定性感知决策，并在故障分类与空气质量建模等应用中表现优异，同时保持了数据隐私与通信效率。

Abstract: We present Personalized Federated Hierarchical Gaussian Processes (pFedHGP) for probabilistic regression and classification when data are distributed across heterogeneous clients. Each client's latent function decomposes into (i) a shared global component, (ii) a client-specific deviation that shares the global kernel structure, and (iii) a flexible local residual. Sparse inducing-variable approximations and federated variational inference keep raw data local while the server synchronizes only low-dimensional statistics for the shared component. Full predictive distributions support uncertainty-aware decisions. In application studies, pFedHGP attains perfect fault classification in press tonnage monitoring using 13.77% of labeled cycles and recovers geographic zones in federated air-quality modeling without centralizing station-level time series. An Instantaneous Linear Mixing Model viewpoint links the hierarchy to multi-output Gaussian processes for correlated sensors.

</details>


### [208] [How to Guide Your Language Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19356)
*Rohit Dilip,Tianrong Chen,Yuyang Wang,David Van Valen,Joshua Susskind,Miguel Angel Bautista*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 probe guidance 方法，利用已有扩散模型冻结的内部状态构建引导信号，替代 autoguidance 中额外的前向传播，并在连续扩散语言模型上取得无条件生成的新最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 autoguidance 方法在推理时需要额外的强模型前向传播，成本高且难以保证弱/强模型动态一致；作者希望以更高效、更可靠的方式实现引导，并理解 autoguidance 的实际工作机制。

Method: 提出 probe guidance：使用扩散模型的冻结内部状态（探针）构建引导信号，原理与 autoguidance 类似，但消除了推理时的额外前向传播，并确保弱模型与强模型共享相似动态。在连续扩散语言模型上应用并基准测试，同时用探针研究弱检查点作为强模型的传统 autoguidance 设置。

Result: probe guidance 在无条件生成上达到新的最先进性能；应用于 1.7B 扩散语言模型时，在多项选择题问答基准上持续提升。研究还发现，在传统 autoguidance 中弱模型必须来自训练的低熵区域。

Conclusion: probe guidance 提供了一种实用且高效的方法来改进扩散语言模型，并揭示了 autoguidance 背后的实际机制——弱模型需处于低熵训练区域，这为理解此前机制不明的 autoguidance 提供了新见解。

Abstract: We introduce a new method to guide flow matching models. Our approach, which we call probe guidance, uses the frozen internal states of an existing diffusion model to construct a guidance signal. This works using a similar principle as autoguidance, but eliminates the need for an additional forward pass at inference time and provides a reliable path to ensure that the weak and strong model share similar dynamics. We apply and benchmark this method on continuous diffusion language models, where probe guidance sets a new state-of-the-art performance on unconditional generation. When applied to a 1.7B diffusion language model, probe guidance consistently improves on multiple choice question answering benchmarks. Using our probes, we study the traditional autoguidance setting where the strong model is a weak checkpoint, and find that the weak model must come from a low-entropy region of training. These findings both provide a practical way to improve diffusion language models and shed light on the actual mechanism behind autoguidance, which is currently poorly understood.

</details>


### [209] [Smart Insole Human Activity Recognition for Continuous Monitoring in Elderly Care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19359)
*Edwin Rios,Antony Garcia,Fengpei Yuan,Xinming Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无线智能鞋垫平台及机器学习流程，通过足底压力和惯性信号识别坐、站、行走与不稳行走四种活动状态；在参与者独立交叉验证下，直方图梯度提升（HGB）使用双侧传感达到0.980的macro-F1，且紧凑1D-CNN未显著优于HGB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 老年人跌倒往往先于活动能力、平衡和姿势转换的变差，因此需要一种低干扰、可穿戴的鞋内传感方案，在训练未见过的个体上仍能可靠推断活动状态，从而为老年人护理中的活动监测与跌倒预防奠定基础。

Method: 开发无线智能鞋垫，每只鞋垫集成16个主动压力传感点与六维IMU（三轴加速度+三轴角速度）；招募15名健康成人以80 Hz采样，将数据分割为重叠窗口。先用分层10折交叉验证筛选窗口长度与候选模型族，再以参与者独立的5折Stratified Group交叉验证（同一参与者的所有窗口置于同一折）获得主性能估计；对比Histogram-Based Gradient Boosting（HGB）与紧凑的1D-CNN。

Result: 在参与者独立5折Stratified Group交叉验证下，HGB对左、右脚的macro-F1分别为0.954和0.959，双侧传感可达0.980；使用相同参与者独立折的紧凑1D-CNN未显著优于HGB（p=0.0625）。

Conclusion: 低剖面鞋内传感能够仅凭压力和IMU测量推断训练中未见过的参与者的活动状态；HGB在识别准确性、稳定性与简单性方面表现良好，为老年人护理中的活动监测和跌倒预防提供了可行基础。

Abstract: Falls in older adults are often preceded by changes in mobility, balance, and postural transitions. This paper presents a wireless smart insole platform and machine-learning workflow for recognizing sitting, standing, walking, and unstable walking from plantar-pressure and inertial signals. Each insole integrates 16 active pressure-sensing locations and a six-dimensional IMU stream consisting of tri-axial acceleration and angular velocity. Data were collected from 15 healthy adults at 80~Hz and segmented into overlapping windows. Window length and candidate model families were first screened with stratified 10-fold cross-validation; the primary performance estimate was then obtained with participant-independent 5-fold Stratified Group cross-validation, ensuring that all windows from a participant remained in a single fold. Under this protocol, Histogram-Based Gradient Boosting (HGB) achieved macro-F1 scores of 0.954 and 0.959 for the left and right feet, respectively, and 0.980 with bilateral sensing. A compact 1D-CNN evaluated with the same participant-independent folds did not significantly outperform HGB ($p=0.0625$). The results show that low-profile footwear sensing can infer activity state from pressure and IMU measurements for participants unseen during training, establishing a basis for activity monitoring and fall prevention in elderly care.

</details>


### [210] [Stiefel Attention: When the Geometry of Transformer Projection Matrices Dominates Optimizer Choice---and When It Does Not](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19363)
*Rubén Darío Guerrero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 作者将注意力中的查询/键投影矩阵约束到 Stiefel 流形上，并用带标量二阶矩、信赖域与极式收缩的 Riemannian Adam 优化，证明该更新在嵌入度量下是最陡下降、梯度尺度无关、良态且 O(d)-等变；其机制在于权重衰减在 St(d,r) 上的黎曼梯度恒为零，使注意力几何在衰减引发的塌缩循环中得以保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力中的 WQ、WK 投影通常被无几何约束的欧氏优化器训练，其几何结构不受控制；作者希望通过对投影施加 Stiefel 流形约束并配套黎曼优化，保护注意力几何免受权重衰减引发的模型内部塌缩循环破坏。

Method: 将 WQ、WK 约束到 Stiefel 流形 St(d,r)，采用 Riemannian Adam 优化：每个 frame 携带一个标量二阶矩、以信赖域封顶步长、用极式（polar）retraction 收缩。通过四个命题证明该更新是嵌入度量下的最陡下降、梯度尺度无关、良态且严格 O(d)-等变（float64 数值验证）；第五个命题给出机制——权重衰减的黎曼梯度在 St(d,r) 上恒为零。实验覆盖 modular arithmetic grokking（单次运行 97.0% vs 基线 61.1% 验证精度）与 CIFAR-10 patches（12 组配对起点 +8.98 pp，t=60.6，12/12），并做消融（尺度自由、投影器、等变性）与负结果（规范移除无法提供动机）。

Result: 数值上四个命题均被 float64 验证成立；grokking 单次运行在 epoch 20000 达 97.0% 验证精度（基线 61.1%，作者声明该端点不稳定、仅作为机制证据而非效应量）；CIFAR-10 patches 上同一规则比基线高 +8.98 pp（t=60.6，12/12 配对起点），且差距随数据增多而扩大。固定步长的黎曼更新对梯度是一次（degree one）的，每步移动量仅为同形 AdamW 矩阵的 24–40 分之一，frame 几乎不离开初始化，完全冻结 frame 仅损失 0.28 pp。消融显示全部增益来自步长尺度自由，投影器与等变性无可测贡献；gauge removal 的负结果说明损失不变方向不携带梯度，无法为该法提供动机。

Conclusion: 将注意力投影约束到 Stiefel 流形并用尺度自由的黎曼步长规则优化，主要收益来自步长尺度自由而非投影器或等变性；其核心机制是权重衰减在 St(d,r) 上黎曼梯度恒为零，从而保护注意力几何不被模型其余部分的衰减塌缩循环所侵蚀。

Abstract: The query and key projections $\WQ,\WK$ in attention are almost always trained by Euclidean optimizers with no constraint on their geometry. We constrain them to the Stiefel manifold and optimize them there with a Riemannian Adam that carries one scalar second moment per frame, caps its step by a trust region, and retracts polarly. Four propositions prove this update is steepest descent in the embedded metric, independent of gradient scale, well conditioned, and exactly $\mathrm{O}(d)$-equivariant, each certified numerically in \texttt{float64}. A fifth supplies the mechanism: weight decay has \emph{identically zero} Riemannian gradient on $\St(d,r)$, since $W = W I_r$ lies in the normal space, so the learned attention geometry survives the collapse cycles that decay drives through the rest of the model. On modular arithmetic grokking, a single run holds $97.0\%$ validation accuracy at epoch 20\,000 against the baseline's $61.1\%$---an unstable endpoint we report as evidence for the mechanism rather than as an effect size. On CIFAR-10 patches the same rule gains $\mathbf{+8.98}$\,pp over 12 paired starts ($t{=}60.6$, $12/12$), and the gap widens with data rather than eroding. The step rule earns this: a fixed-step Riemannian update is degree one in the gradient, so it moves $24$--$40\times$ less per step than an identically shaped AdamW matrix---its frames barely leave their initialization, and freezing them outright costs only $0.28$\,pp. An ablation credits the whole gain to making the step scale free, and nothing measurable to the projector or to equivariance. A negative result sharpens the account: gauge removal cannot motivate the method, because a direction along which the loss is invariant carries no gradient at all.

</details>


### [211] [Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM) Improves Classification Accuracy of Bacterial Raman Spectral Data Enabling Portable Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19453)
*Kaitlin Zareno,Jarett Dewbury,Siamak K. Sorooshyari,Hossein Mobahi,Loza F. Tadesse*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出用锐度感知最小化（SAM）优化器来提升拉曼光谱分类模型在有限临床数据集上的泛化能力，解决了现有算法泛化性差和对复杂预处理依赖的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抗菌素耐药性预计到2050年每年将导致1000万人死亡，资源有限地区受影响最大。拉曼光谱是一种快速、便携的病原体诊断方法，可在数小时内完成抗生素耐药性测试（金标准方法需数天）。然而，现有拉曼光谱分析算法存在两大局限：1）在有限数据集上难以泛化到多样化的患者群体；2）由于拉曼光谱数据质量低，需要特征提取等非平凡的预处理步骤，导致模型复杂度增加。

Method: 本文采用锐度感知最小化（Sharpness-Aware Minimization, SAM）优化器，通过在临床细菌分离株分类任务中对多样的超参数组合进行训练，来增强模型的泛化能力，替代传统的Adam优化器。

Result: 实验结果表明，与传统优化器Adam相比，SAM在光谱分类任务的单个数据划分上准确率最高提升10.5%，在所有划分上的平均准确率提升2.7%。

Conclusion: SAM能够显著提升拉曼光谱分类模型的性能，展现了其在推动AI驱动的拉曼光谱工具在临床应用中的潜力。

Abstract: Antimicrobial resistance is expected to claim 10 million lives per year by 2050, and resource-limited regions are most affected. Raman spectroscopy is a novel pathogen diagnostic approach promising rapid and portable antibiotic resistance testing within a few hours, compared to days when using gold standard methods. However, current algorithms for Raman spectra analysis 1) are unable to generalize well on limited datasets across diverse patient populations and 2) require increased complexity due to the necessity of non-trivial pre-processing steps, such as feature extraction, which are essential to mitigate the low-quality nature of Raman spectral data. In this work, we address these limitations using Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM) to enhance model generalization across a diverse array of hyperparameters in clinical bacterial isolate classification tasks. We demonstrate that SAM achieves accuracy improvements of up to 10.5% on a single split, and an increase in average accuracy of 2.7% across all splits in spectral classification tasks over the traditional optimizer, Adam. These results display the capability of SAM to advance the clinical application of AI-powered Raman spectroscopy tools.

</details>


### [212] [Machine-Learning Assessment of the Predictive Value of Inflammatory Biomarkers for Cognitive Impairment in an Older Hispanic Adult Cohort](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19374)
*Antony Garcia,Gabrielle Britton,Alcibiades Villarreal,Diana Oviedo,Giselle Rangel,Xinming Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对小样本临床表格数据，作者构建了一种防泄漏的阈值似然朴素贝叶斯分类器，在 PARI-HD 队列（n=165）中发现炎症因子 I-309/CCL1 对认知障碍预测具有稳健的增量价值，而其他 17 个候选标志物不具备可靠的增量预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小样本临床表格数据下，深度学习方法不实用，集成模型难以解释；且统计显著不等于预测有用，需要一个既可解释又能严格评估特征增量预测价值的方法。

Method: 基于 PARI-HD 队列（n=165），实现防泄漏的阈值似然伯努利/类别朴素贝叶斯（BNB/CNB）分类器。在每个训练折内，连续预测变量通过监督式卡方推导的状态离散化；收入以类别似然进入模型。所有依赖数据的步骤均在 30 次重复的分层 10 折交叉验证内部完成。以 ROC-AUC 评估，并用配对 DeLong 检验检验增量显著性；在预先指定的主分析中用固定分区 DeLong 检验，再用 200 个随机分区评估稳健性；对 18 个候选标志物进行探索性检验并用 Benjamini-Hochberg 校正（q 值）。

Result: 人口统计学基线 ROC-AUC 为 0.630 ± 0.017。I-309（CCL1）是最主要的增量特征，使 AUC 提升 0.110，且在 100% 的重复中配对 DeLong 检验 p<0.05。预先指定的主分析中，I-309 的固定分区 DeLong p=0.0018；200 个随机分区的稳健性评估中 p 值中位数为 0.0011。探索性分析中 I-309 在冻结分区上 Benjamini-Hochberg 校正 q=0.032，且 85% 的随机分区满足 q<0.05，而其他标志物均未显示可靠的增量预测价值。

Conclusion: 由于拟合模型是可检查的阈值与类条件概率表，结果将 I-309/CCL1 识别为认知障碍表格化预测的可解释候选特征，但其结论仍需外部验证确认。

Abstract: Small clinical tabular datasets require interpretable machine learning because deep learning is often impractical and ensemble models can be difficult to inspect. A key pitfall is that statistical significance does not necessarily imply predictive utility. Using data from the Panama Aging Research Initiative--Health Disparities (PARI-HD) cohort (n=165), we implemented a leakage-safe threshold-likelihood Bernoulli/Categorical Naive Bayes (BNB/CNB) classifier. Within every training fold, each continuous predictor was reduced to a supervised chi-square-derived state, while income entered the model through a categorical likelihood. All data-dependent steps were performed within repeated stratified 10-fold cross-validation with 30 repeats. The demographic baseline achieved a ROC-AUC of 0.630 +/- 0.017. I-309 (CCL1) was the dominant incremental feature, increasing AUC by 0.110, with paired DeLong tests yielding p<0.05 in 100% of repeats. In the pre-specified primary analysis, I-309 produced a fixed-partition DeLong p=0.0018, with robustness assessed across 200 random partitions, where the median p-value was 0.0011. Within the exploratory family of 18 candidate markers, I-309 achieved a Benjamini-Hochberg-adjusted q=0.032 on the frozen partition and satisfied q<0.05 in 85% of random partitions, whereas no other marker demonstrated reliable incremental predictive value. Because the fitted model is an inspectable table of thresholds and class-conditional probabilities, these results identify I-309/CCL1 as an interpretable candidate feature for tabular prediction of cognitive impairment, pending external validation.

</details>


### [213] [Compressed Active Subspaces for Scalable Bayesian Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19539)
*Thomas Flynn,Sanket Jantre,Byung-Jun Yoon,Kibaek Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出压缩主动子空间（CAS）方法，通过结构化的等距嵌入先对高维参数进行压缩，再在降维后的参数化空间中构建主动子空间，从而大幅降低存储需求，使大规模模型的贝叶斯推断成为可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动子空间方法能够量化高维模型的预测不确定性，但构建过程需存储大量全维模型梯度，内存开销随模型规模增大而变得不可承受，限制了其在大模型上的应用。

Method: 提出CAS方法：先用结构化的等距嵌入将模型参数映射到压缩空间，再在该降维参数化内构建主动子空间，并沿影响力最大的参数方向进行推断，从而显著减少主动子空间构建所需的内存。

Result: 在规模递增的神经网络上验证了CAS的可扩展性，实验结果表明确保了预测性能，同时保持了稳健的不确定性估计，且内存开销大幅降低。

Conclusion: CAS为传统主动子空间方法难以处理的大规模模型提供了一种可扩展的贝叶斯推断方案，在降低内存需求的同时维持了预测精度与不确定性量化的可靠性。

Abstract: Active subspace methods provide a framework for quantifying predictive uncertainty in high-dimensional models by identifying and performing inference along parameter directions that have the greatest influence on the model output. However, the construction of active subspaces requires storing many full-dimensional model gradients, which becomes prohibitive as model size increases. We address this limitation by proposing Compressed Active Subspaces (CAS), a scalable approach that first maps the model parameters to a compressed space using a structured isometric embedding and then constructs the active subspace within this reduced parameterization. Our approach substantially reduces the memory required for active subspace construction and enables Bayesian inference for large models where standard active subspace methods become impractical. We demonstrate the scalability of CAS on neural networks of increasing size while maintaining predictive performance and robust uncertainty estimates.

</details>


### [214] [FCx: An algorithm for finding Feasible Counterfactual Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19383)
*Kleopatra Markou,Vana Kalogeraki,Dimitrios Gunopulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Feasible Counterfactual Explanations（FCx），一种基于改进变分自编码器（VAE）的方法，用于生成同时满足现实性、低成本和可行性的反事实解释，并通过多因子损失函数优化，在四个公开数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反事实解释方法虽然能生成现实且低成本的改动，但往往忽视可行性，会建议不可执行的修改（如改变个人种族以获得工作机会），或提出与未来变化不兼容的修改。因此需要一种显式强制可行性的反事实解释生成方法。

Method: 提出 FCx 方法，基于改进的 VAE，用多因子损失函数优化。同时支持两类可行性约束：由用户领域知识指定的硬约束，以及通过因果推断从数据集中自动推断的软约束。修改成本用接近度（数值绝对变化）和稀疏度（改变特征的数量）衡量；现实性用局部离群因子（LOF）进行密度估计来度量，保证反事实落在稠密区域。

Result: 在四个公开数据集上的广泛实验表明，该方法在多个指标上达到最先进水平，同时保证可行性。

Conclusion: FCx 是首个能高效生成同时具备现实性、低成本和可行性的反事实解释方法，通过显式建模硬、软可行性约束，在保证性能的前提下解决了现有方法可行性不足的问题。

Abstract: Counterfactual (CF) explanations identify changes that alter an input's classification. While existing methods produce realistic and low-cost CFs, they often fail to ensure feasibility, by suggesting non-constructive modifications or incompatible with future changes (e.g., changing an individual's race to secure a job offer). We introduce a refinement of CF explanations that explicitly enforces feasibility. Our approach is the first to efficiently generate CFs that are realistic, low-cost and feasible. We accommodate both hard feasible constraints, specified by domain knowledge users, and soft feasible constraints, inferred automatically via causal inference from the dataset. Our method, Feasible Counterfactual Explanations (FCx), is based on a modified Variational Autoencoder (VAE) optimized with a multi-factor loss function. We measure the cost of a change based on the absolute change in values (proximity) as well as the number of features changed (sparsity) while realism is measured based on the LOF for density estimation, guaranteeing that CFs reside in densely populated regions. Extensive experiments on four public datasets show that our approach matches state-of-the-art performance across multiple metrics while guaranteeing feasibility.

</details>


### [215] [Learn Your Own Thoughts: Abstract Token Curriculum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19717)
*Khashayar Gatmiry,Avrajit Ghosh,Parsa Mirtaheri,Jason D. Lee,Nika Haghtalab,Emmanuel Abbe,Peter Bartlett*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出抽象令牌课程（ATC），一种无需显式思维令牌监督即可在连续表示空间中诱导有效中间推理表示的课程学习框架，并从理论与实验两方面证明其优于以往连续思维训练方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有思维链（CoT）方法虽使大模型具备强大推理能力，但要求对思维令牌进行显式监督，依赖丰富且任务特定的数据，成本高且难以泛化。作者希望在不依赖直接监督或人工设计草稿的情况下，激发模型的连续中间表示（内部"思维"）。

Method: 提出抽象令牌课程（Abstract Token Curriculum, ATC）框架：通过一系列复杂度递增的问题分布逐步提高任务难度，使模型在连续表示空间内自发形成内部抽象"思维"。理论上，针对单层 softmax 注意力学习奇偶校验函数，证明 ATC 下注意力会自然聚焦于上下文中提供"最易路径"的 CoT 令牌；实验上，在图可达性与算术学习任务上验证 ATC 的有效性。

Result: 理论上证明 ATC 使注意力聚焦于最易预测下一令牌的 CoT 令牌；实验表明 ATC 在图可达性和算术学习任务上均表现有效，且优于以往连续思维训练方法。

Conclusion: ATC 作为一种课程学习框架，能够在不依赖显式监督或人工草稿设计的情况下有效引出连续中间表示，为训练模型内部"思维"提供了兼具理论支撑与实验验证的新途径。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable reasoning capabilities by utilizing chain-of-thought (CoT) as a scratchpad for intermediate stages of thinking. However, CoT techniques require explicit supervision on thinking tokens, which requires rich, task-specific data. In this work, we propose Abstract Token Curriculum (ATC), a novel curriculum learning framework that elicits effective continuous intermediate representations without direct supervision or manual scratchpad design. ATC gradually increases problem complexity through a sequence of distributions, training the model to develop internal abstract ``thoughts'' in the continuous representation space. This paper provides both theoretical and experimental evidence for the benefits of ATC and its advantages over previous methods for training continuous thoughts. Theoretically, we show that for learning parity functions with single-layer softmax attention using ATC, attention naturally focuses on the CoT tokens in the context that provide the ``easiest path'' to predicting the next token. Experimentally, we show ATC's effectiveness on graph reachability and arithmetic learning tasks.

</details>


### [216] [Improving Offline Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning via Selective Reward Stimulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19414)
*Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RSIQL，一种非层次的离线目标条件强化学习方法，通过对轨迹中取得进展的中间状态施加额外奖励刺激，缓解稀疏奖励与长时程依赖下的延迟目标问题，在保持扁平策略结构的同时取得与层次方法相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线目标条件强化学习在稀疏奖励和长时程依赖下困难，目标完成信息在时间上与使成功成为可能的早期决策相距遥远，且离线价值估计引入额外误差，导致目标导向的价值分离相对局部估计误差变小，从而削弱学习信号。

Method: 从奖励传播角度分析延迟目标设置中的问题后，提出Reward Stimulation Implicit Q-Learning (RSIQL)：利用辅助的目标条件价值函数识别离线轨迹中被估计为向目标取得进展的中间状态，并在这些状态施加额外奖励刺激，提供延迟更短的训练监督。该方法不学习独立的高层子目标策略，保持扁平的非层次策略结构。

Result: 在D4RL目标到达基准和OGBench上的实验表明，RSIQL平均上优于目标条件IQL，并达到与层次化离线目标条件方法有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: RSIQL通过奖励刺激缓解延迟目标下的稀疏奖励问题，以简单扁平结构取得与层次方法相近的性能，验证了在中间进展状态注入奖励信号的有效性。

Abstract: Goal-conditioned reinforcement learning aims to learn policies that reach specified goals, but remains challenging in offline settings with sparse rewards and long-horizon dependencies. In such settings, goal-completion information can be temporally distant from the early decisions that enable success, while offline value estimation introduces additional error. We study this issue from a reward-propagation perspective and show, in a stylized delayed-goal setting, how goal-directed value separation can become small relative to local estimation error. Motivated by this analysis, we propose Reward Stimulation Implicit Q-Learning (RSIQL), a simple non-hierarchical method that introduces additional reward signals at progress-making intermediate states in offline trajectories. RSIQL uses an auxiliary goal-conditioned value function to identify intermediate states estimated to make progress toward the goal and applies reward stimulation to provide less-delayed training supervision. Unlike hierarchical methods, RSIQL does not learn a separate high-level subgoal policy. Experiments on D4RL goal-reaching benchmarks and OGBench show that RSIQL improves over goal-conditioned IQL on average and achieves performance competitive with hierarchical offline goal-conditioned methods, while retaining a simple flat policy structure.

</details>


### [217] [Bayesian Optimization with Rich Auxiliary Information via LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19437)
*Tejus Gupta,Efe Mert Karagözlü,Rohit Sonker,Barnabás Póczos,Jeff Schnieder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明大语言模型能有效利用优化问题中的丰富辅助信息（如训练曲线、专家笔记、先验知识）来指导贝叶斯优化，并提出三种将辅助信息融入BO的方法，在多个基准和真实核聚变任务上均优于标准BO和现有LLM优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的优化问题往往包含远比单纯函数评估更丰富的信息（如超参优化中的训练曲线、科学实验中的专家笔记和图像、关于最优解位置的先验知识），但标准贝叶斯优化仅依赖函数评估，未能利用这些辅助信息，导致性能受限。

Method: 提出三种利用大语言模型将辅助信息融入贝叶斯优化的方法。核心思路是通过LLM对丰富辅助信息进行理解和编码，将其转化为可指导优化过程的先验或信号，从而在采集过程中更有效地引导搜索。

Result: 在超参数优化基准测试和一项真实世界核聚变优化任务上，所提出的方法一致优于标准贝叶斯优化以及现有的基于LLM的优化方法，验证了LLM利用丰富辅助信息进行贝叶斯优化的有效性。

Conclusion: 大语言模型能够有效利用丰富辅助信息来提升贝叶斯优化的性能，展示了LLM在辅助信息丰富的真实优化问题中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Bayesian Optimization (BO) is widely used for optimizing expensive black-box functions, yet many real-world optimization problems contain substantially richer information than function evaluations alone. Examples include training curves in hyperparameter optimization, expert notes and images in scientific experimentation, and prior knowledge about where optima may lie. We show that large language models (LLMs) can effectively leverage such rich auxiliary information to guide optimization. Motivated by these findings, we develop three methods for incorporating auxiliary information into BO using LLMs. Across hyperparameter optimization benchmarks and a real-world nuclear fusion optimization task, our methods consistently outperform both standard BO and existing LLM-based optimization approaches. Our results demonstrate the effectiveness of LLMs for leveraging rich auxiliary information in BO.

</details>


### [218] [When Does Retrieval Help Time-Series Forecasting?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20193)
*Mert Onur Cakiroglu,Elham Buxton,Mehmet Dalkilic,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示检索插件（retrieval plug-ins）为时序预测带来的提升主要源于操作点（窗口长度 S 与主导季节周期 L 的关系），而非各插件声称的机制；作者通过分层评估、简单对照实验和合成扫描证明了这一点，并提出两个可解释统计量用于部署前筛查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究普遍报告检索插件能提升深度预测器性能，但各自归因于不同的机制，且标准评测协议从不改变窗口长度与季节周期的关系，导致增益来源未被真正辨析。作者试图厘清这些增益究竟来自插件的机制，还是来自操作点的选择。

Method: 作者采用分层评估策略，按窗口长度 S 与主导周期 L 的关系对评测结果分层；引入"重复上一观测周期"的简单对照（naive control）与六个标准骨干网络及最强插件对比；在七个基准上做聚合比较；通过受控合成实验扫描 horizon、period、window 三个维度并计算相关系数；用"无相位可恢复"的配对对照检验相位饥饿（phase starvation）假设；对比零样本预训练基础模型与训练后骨干网络；并构建趋势检验与陈旧率两个可解释统计量，用留一数据集评估预测逐单元增益的正负号。

Result: 在 S=12 时，重复上一周期的简单对照在七个基准中的四个上总体击败六个标准骨干，MSE 增益达 8%–44%；它战胜所运行的最强插件（ETTm1），并在 ECL 上与之持平，在三个训练集频谱缺乏集中共享周期的数据集上最多差 25%。合成扫描显示增益边界与周期相关（相关系数 +0.71），与 horizon 弱相关（-0.23）。无相位对照几乎消除了该效应，支持相位饥饿解释。零样本基础模型在周期基准上落后训练后骨干 22%–50%。精确查找与图扩散表现相当，说明收益来自查询记录本身而非其上机制。两个统计量在留一数据集评估下以 0.76 准确率预测逐单元增益正负号，优于 0.69 的多数规则，而 22 特征集成模型仅达 0.57。

Conclusion: 检索插件的收益主要归因于操作点（窗口长度与主导周期的关系）而非插件机制；作者贡献在于提出该"状态图"（regime map）、揭示它的评测协议，以及两个在部署前筛查的可解释统计量，并未提出新插件。

Abstract: Retrieval plug-ins supply a deep forecaster with information its lookback window cannot carry. Published evaluations report consistent gains, and each credits its own mechanism. We show that the benefit belongs instead to the operating point: the relation between window length $S$ and dominant seasonal period $L$, an axis the standard protocol never varies. Stratifying the evaluation by that relation exposes the regime. At $S{=}12$, a simple control that repeats the last observed period beats the six standard backbones, in aggregate, on four of seven benchmarks by $8\%$ to $44\%$ of MSE. It beats the strongest plug-in we run on ETTm1 and matches it on ECL. It is worse by up to $25\%$ on the three datasets whose training-split spectra lack a concentrated, shared period. A controlled synthetic sweep of horizon, period, and window shows the benefit boundary tracks the period (correlation $+0.71$), not the horizon ($-0.23$). A paired control with no phase to recover nearly erases the effect, consistent with phase starvation. Zero-shot pretraining does not escape it: a foundation model trails trained backbones by $22\%$ to $50\%$ on the periodic benchmarks. Within our instrument, exact lookup matches graph diffusion: the payoff is consulting the record, not the machinery on top. Two interpretable statistics, a trend test and a staleness rate, predict the sign of the per-cell benefit at $0.76$ accuracy under leave-one-dataset-out evaluation, a suggestive margin over the $0.69$ majority rule, where a 22-feature stack manages $0.57$. We propose no new plug-in. The contribution is the regime map, the protocol that reveals it, and two statistics that screen it before deployment. Code: https://github.com/KurbanIntelligenceLab/retrieval-regime.

</details>


### [219] [Enhanced Agriculture-informed Neural Network by Domain Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19466)
*Ci Lin,Futong Li,Rose Chong-Wu,Tet Yeap,Iluju Kiringa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出知识增强型农业神经网络（KAINN），在农业神经网络（AINN）基础上融入肥料扩散、土壤呼吸和充水孔隙度等物理知识，显著提升了农业N₂O排放预测的精度、可解释性与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业N₂O排放预测对评估环境影响和支持可持续农业至关重要，但由于其受土壤性质、气候、生化过程和管理措施等多因素复杂交互影响，且高质量观测数据有限，纯数据驱动的深度学习模型虽能拟合非线性关系，却缺乏物理可解释性、跨环境泛化能力差，因此需要引入领域知识来弥补这些不足。

Method: 提出KAINN，一种神经-机理混合框架，在原有AINN基础上引入肥料扩散、土壤呼吸和充水孔隙度等物理知识；分别采用CNN、LSTM和Transformer三种架构，并在多个生长季和多种输入特征配置下进行验证与对比。

Result: KAINN相比纯数据驱动模型和原始AINN普遍取得更低的RMSE、MAE和更高的R²；对学习到的接口分析显示，参数轨迹更平滑、物理一致性更强，且不确定性降低。

Conclusion: 将环境知识融入神经网络能有效提升农业N₂O排放预测的可靠性、可解释性和泛化能力，验证了神经-机理混合建模方法的有效性。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of nitrous oxide (N2O) emissions from agriculture is important for assessing environmental impacts and supporting sustainable farming. However, prediction remains difficult because N2O emissions result from complex interactions among soil properties, climate, biochemical processes, and management practices, while high-quality observations are limited. Deep learning models can capture nonlinear relationships but often lack physical interpretability and may generalize poorly across environmental conditions. We propose the Knowledge-enhanced Agriculture-informed Neural Network (KAINN), a hybrid neural-mechanistic framework that extends the Agriculture-informed Neural Network by incorporating domain knowledge about fertilizer diffusion, soil respiration, and water-filled porosity. We evaluate KAINN using CNN, LSTM, and Transformer architectures across multiple growing seasons and input-feature configurations. The results show that KAINN generally provides lower root mean square error and mean absolute error and higher R-squared values than purely data-driven models and the original AINN. Analysis of the learned interfaces also shows smoother and more physically consistent parameter trajectories with reduced uncertainty. These findings demonstrate that incorporating environmental knowledge into neural networks can improve the reliability, interpretability, and generalization of agricultural N2O-emission predictions.

</details>


### [220] [The Bias of Nonlinear Two-Time-scale Stochastic Approximation under Constant Step-Sizes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20409)
*Djamel Rassem Lamouri,Dorian Baudry,Nicolas Gast*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为非线性双时间尺度随机逼近（TTSA）算法建立了有限时间收敛保证。在步长满足 α≫β 及标准稳定性、正则性与马尔可夫噪声假设下，作者给出了两个迭代量围绕其极限均衡点的均方误差与偏差上界，其量级为 O(α+β²/α²)，并在 β≤α^(3/2) 时证明了该界的紧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双时间尺度随机逼近是分析强化学习、优化与随机控制中耦合迭代算法的基本工具，但非线性双时间尺度方案在恒定步长下的有限时间保证一直难以获得。现有工作主要覆盖线性 TTSA，非线性情形缺乏相应的误差界与紧性结果，因此需要填补这一空白并揭示非线性带来的新效应。

Method: 作者研究步长 α≫β 的非线性 TTSA，在标准稳定性假设、正则性假设及马尔可夫噪声假设下进行分析。核心做法是将误差分解为初值条件、快时间尺度跟踪误差、马尔可夫依赖性与时间尺度耦合等若干来源分别处理，从而厘清 β²/α² 项的产生机制，并最终联合给出均方误差与偏差的上界。为证明紧性，论文还构造了下界匹配的论证，使其在 β≤α^(3/2) 时成立。

Result: 论文证明两个迭代量围绕极限均衡点的均方误差与偏差上界为 O(α+β²/α²)，并在 β≤α^(3/2) 条件下证明该界是紧的（即不可再改进）。误差分解表明 β²/α² 项并非源于快时间尺度跟踪误差或初值效应，而是源自非线性动态下特有的有限时间耦合效应。这一结果与既有线性 TTSA 情形存在定性差异：非线性动态引入了线性情形中不存在的额外有限时间效应。

Conclusion: 本文为恒定步长下的非线性双时间尺度随机逼近首次给出了量级为 O(α+β²/α²) 的有限时间均方误差与偏差界，并证明了其紧性。通过精细的误差来源分解，论文厘清了 β²/α² 项的物理来源，并揭示非线性动态相较线性情形的定性新效应，为后续非线性 TTSA 的理论分析提供了基础。

Abstract: Two-timescale stochastic approximation (TTSA) is a fundamental tool for analyzing coupled iterative algorithms in reinforcement learning, optimization, and stochastic control. However, finite-time guarantees for nonlinear two-timescale schemes remain difficult to obtain, especially under constant step-sizes. In this paper, we study nonlinear TTSA with step-sizes $α\ggβ$. Under standard stability, regularity, and Markovian noise assumptions, we upper bound the mean-squared error and the bias of both iterates around their limiting equilibria. Our bounds scale as $O(α+β^2/α^2)$, which we prove to be tight when $β\leα^{3/2}$. The analysis separates the contributions of initial conditions, fast-timescale tracking error, Markovian dependence, and timescale coupling, thereby clarifying the origin of the $β^2/α^2$ term. Our results reveal qualitative differences from the linear TTSA setting previously studied, showing that nonlinear dynamics introduce additional finite-time effects that are absent in the linear case.

</details>


### [221] [Search at the Cost of Sampling: Nearly-Instant Latent Space Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19476)
*Donney Fan,Colin Doumont,Aleksandra Kalisz,Paul Duckworth,Jacob R. Gardner,Henry Moss,Geoff Pleiss*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种实用的贝叶斯优化方法，利用球面上高维潜变量的线性模型及近闭式解，将计算速度提升至少100倍，使其适用于从头设计（de novo）发现流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型在从头发现流程中被大规模使用，虽然贝叶斯优化（BO）非常契合这种用历史评估指导新提议的场景，但其顺序决策所需的计算开销在实际虚拟筛选成本相对低廉的情况下成为瓶颈，导致BO难以落地。作者旨在降低计算成本，使BO成为该场景下实用的即插即用方案。

Method: 利用线性模型受限于球形域（高维潜变量集中分布处）这一独特组合，基于近期证明线性代理模型合理性的工作，推导出利用球对称性的近闭式（nearly closed-form）代理建模与采集函数的解，从而大幅降低BO每步决策的计算量。

Result: 相比最先进的基线方法，实现至少100倍的加速，同时在分子生成和图像生成基准上取得匹配或更优的性能。

Conclusion: 该方法使贝叶斯优化成为从头设计流程中实用的即插即用组件，解决了此前因速度过慢而无法考虑使用BO的问题。

Abstract: Generative models are increasingly central to many de novo discovery pipelines, in which designs are generated at scale and filtered through virtual screens to determine a set of candidates to experimentally validate. While Bayesian optimization (BO) is a natural fit for this setting, as it uses past evaluations to guide future proposals, the computational overhead required for its sequential decision-making becomes a bottleneck when virtual screens are relatively cheap. We make BO practical in this regime by exploiting the unique combination of a linear model constrained to a spherical domain where high-dimensional latents concentrate. We build off recent work justifying the use of linear surrogates, while deriving nearly closed-form solutions to the surrogate modelling and acquisition problems that exploit spherical symmetry. The result is at least a 100x speedup over state-of-the art baselines, with matching or improved performance across molecular and image generation benchmarks. Altogether, our method makes BO a practical drop-in for de novo pipelines where it was previously too slow to consider.

</details>


### [222] [Parallelism, critical windows, and separations among diffusion language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20539)
*Sitan Chen,Liye Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次在并行能力上对三类主流扩散大语言模型（均匀扩散、高斯扩散、掩码扩散）进行细粒度比较，证明均匀与高斯扩散能以与分布“对偶总相关”成比例的采样步数生成文本，并首次给出掩码扩散在并行性上分离开的严格证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型以并行生成为卖点，但掩码、均匀、高斯三大范式在并行能力上的原理性比较仍缺乏系统理解；亟需从理论上厘清它们的采样复杂度差异及其来源。

Method: 作者以“对偶总相关”这一内在复杂度度量为主线，分析三种扩散范式的采样前向传播次数；针对一类随机经验测度，刻画其 $\widetilde{\Theta}(\sqrt{d})$ 的必要与充分条件，并构造近似得分预言机（score oracle）证明掩码扩散需 $\widetilde{\Omega}(d)$ 步，从而建立可证明的并行性分离。

Result: 均匀与高斯扩散能以与对偶总相关成比例的步数采样（此前仅掩码扩散已知可做到）；对某类随机经验测度，均匀/高斯扩散需且仅需 $\widetilde{\Theta}(\sqrt{d})$ 次前向传播，而掩码扩散在存在近似得分预言机时需 $\widetilde{\Omega}(d)$ 次，构成三种范式间首个可证明的并行性分离。

Conclusion: 抑制掩码扩散并行的并非“必须逐 token 确定取值”的直觉，而是其采样中的“关键窗口”渐近地比均匀与高斯扩散更窄，导致掩码扩散并行性更差。这为 dLLM 范式的选择与设计提供了新的理论依据。

Abstract: A popular selling point of diffusion large language models (dLLMs) is their capacity for parallelism: the ability to generate sequences of text far more efficiently than autoregressive models, which require one forward pass per token. Yet among the many competing paradigms for dLLMs, from masked to uniform to Gaussian diffusion, principled understanding of how these different proposals compare in parallelism remains limited. In this work, we initiate a fine-grained comparison of the capacity for parallelism among these three leading approaches and prove the following:
  - Uniform and Gaussian diffusion can sample in a number of forward passes which scales with the dual total correlation of the underlying distribution, a measure of intrinsic complexity which can be much smaller than the context length. Previously, it was only known how to achieve this using masked diffusion.
  - For a certain family of random empirical measures, we show that $\widetildeΘ(\sqrt{d})$ forward passes are necessary and sufficient to sample using uniform or Gaussian diffusion, yet there exist approximate score oracles for which $\widetildeΩ(d)$ forward passes are needed for masked diffusion. This establishes the first provable separation in parallelism between the three prevailing dLLM paradigms.
  Contrary to popular intuition that masked diffusions are harder to parallelize because they must commit to token values, the latter separation instead comes from the fact that the critical windows in masked diffusion sampling are asymptotically narrower than those in uniform and Gaussian diffusion sampling.

</details>


### [223] [Sample Count Is Not Enough: Candidate-Generation Strategy Shapes the Energy and Performance of LLM Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19499)
*Mobina Kashaniyan,Ali Jannesari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究测试时缩放中多候选生成的计算成本问题，指出仅用候选数量N描述推理预算是不充分的，生成调度方式（批次大小与调用次数）显著影响系统成本，并建议评估时同时报告生成调度与GPU系统指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于采样的测试时缩放通过生成并组合多个候选回答来提升大语言模型推理能力，现有工作通常用候选数量N描述推理预算。但N只说明生成了多少候选，未描述这些候选是如何被执行的（一次大批次调用还是多次小批次串行调用），因此无法全面刻画实际系统成本。

Method: 作者以Phi-3-mini和Qwen2.5-1.5B在500条GSM8K提示上进行实验：首先研究N从1增至8对推理准确率的影响；然后固定N=8，比较四种生成调度方式（1x8、2x4、4x2、8x1，axb表示a次调用、每次b个候选），测量延迟、吞吐量、GPU小时数和GPU设备总能耗。实验在A100 GPU上进行，并通过三个独立调度的A100节点及短输出SciQ/V100实验进行验证。

Result: N从1增至8使Phi-3-mini准确率提升8.4个百分点，Qwen2.5-1.5B提升18.4个百分点。固定N=8时，八次串行调用相比一次包含8个候选的批量调用，GPU设备总能耗为4.64-4.86倍，P95延迟为5.77-6.12倍。该模式在三个独立调度的A100节点以及SciQ/V100实验（短输出）中一致出现。

Conclusion: 仅靠候选数量不足以描述多候选测试时缩放的系统成本。当候选相互独立且内存允许时，更少的生成调用配以更大的批次规模更加高效。因此，评估应不仅报告候选数量和准确率，还应报告生成调度方式及GPU级别的系统指标。

Abstract: Test-time scaling can improve large language model reasoning by generating and combining multiple candidate responses. In sampling-based methods, the inference budget is often described by the number of generated candidates, N. However, N tells us how many candidates are generated, not how they are executed. The same candidate budget can be produced in one batched generation call or split across several sequential calls with smaller batch sizes. We first study the effect of increasing N on reasoning accuracy using Phi-3-mini and Qwen2.5-1.5B on 500 GSM8K prompts. As expected, increasing N from 1 to 8 improves accuracy by 8.4 percentage points for Phi-3-mini and 18.4 points for Qwen2.5-1.5B. However, accuracy alone does not show the systems cost of using a larger candidate budget. We therefore fix N = 8 and compare four generation schedules: 1x8, 2x4, 4x2, and 8x1, where axb denotes a generation calls with b candidates per call. We measure latency, throughput, GPU-hours, and gross GPU-device energy while keeping the total candidate count fixed. On A100 GPUs, eight serial calls use 4.64-4.86x as much gross GPU-device energy and have 5.77-6.12x the P95 latency of one batched call with eight candidates. The same pattern appears across three independently scheduled A100 nodes per model and in short-output SciQ/V100 experiments. These results show that candidate count alone is not enough to describe the systems cost of multi-candidate test-time scaling. When candidates are independent and memory allows it, fewer generation calls with larger batch sizes are more efficient. Evaluations should therefore report not only candidate count and accuracy, but also generation schedule and GPU-level systems metrics.

</details>


### [224] [Epidemiological Causal Graph Identification: Challenges, Identifiability and Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20676)
*Sambit Mishra,Yingying Wang,Christine K. Johnson,Urbashi Mitra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究混合数据（序数变量与单参数指数族变量）在 DAG 中的因果方向可辨识性，证明了序数-指数族节点间边方向在通用参数下具有分布可辨识性，并提出了基于打分的穷举搜索与基于 DAGMA 的掩码连续优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从观测数据中发现因果关系是统计与机器学习的核心问题，但在无干预条件下确定因果方向需要结构性假设。现有可辨识性研究主要聚焦于加性噪声模型下的连续变量，忽视了包含序数尺度、计数及连续测量的混合数据集。

Method: 本文研究有向无环图（DAG）中的因果发现，节点服从序数分布（通过有序 Logit 模型）或正则单参数指数族分布；证明序数节点与指数族节点之间边方向在通用参数取值下具有分布可辨识性；计算上提出基于打分的穷举搜索，以及利用 DAGMA 的掩码连续优化框架以处理较大图。

Result: 理论结果将先前序数-泊松的研究推广到更广泛的指数族；数值实验验证了理论，能够在马尔可夫等价类内恢复经典结构方程模型无法辨识的边方向。

Conclusion: 本文扩展了混合数据因果发现的可辨识性理论，从序数-泊松推广到一般单参数指数族，并通过穷举搜索与 DAGMA 掩码连续优化提供了可行的计算方案，数值结果支持理论结论。

Abstract: Causal discovery from observational data is fundamental to statistics and machine learning, yet determining causal direction without interventions necessitates structural assumptions. Existing identifiability research primarily focuses on continuous variables under additive noise models, often neglecting mixed datasets containing ordinal scales, counts, and continuous measurements. This paper investigates causal discovery in Directed Acyclic Graphs (DAGs) where nodes follow either an ordinal distribution (via an ordered logit model) or a regular one-parameter exponential family distribution. We prove that the edge direction between an ordinal and an exponential family node is distributionally identifiable for generic parameter values. Our findings generalize previous Ordinal-Poisson results to the broader exponential family. Computationally, we introduce a score-based exhaustive search and a masked continuous optimization framework using DAGMA for larger graphs. Numerical results validate the theory, recovering edge orientations within a Markov equivalence class that are unidentifiable under classical structural equation models.

</details>


### [225] [LSTM-UT and Recurrent-Depth Transformers on Cellular Automata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19521)
*Aras Kavuncu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较了三种循环深度Transformer（BUT、CoTFormer、LSTM-UT），发现CoTFormer的扩展注意力缓存会损害深度外推与延迟回忆能力，而有界门控记忆的LSTM-UT在这两项任务上表现最佳，说明有界门控记忆是重复计算与后续检索的有效归纳偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环深度Transformer都重复使用共享计算，但在跨步骤保留信息的方式上各不相同。需要厘清不同信息保留机制（仅当前隐藏状态、扩展注意力缓存、有界门控记忆）如何影响深度外推与后续检索能力。

Method: 对比三种模型：BUT 仅携带当前隐藏状态；CoTFormer 额外保留不断扩大的注意力缓存；新提出的 LSTM-UT 采用有界门控记忆。在规则30元胞自动机上测试深度外推，并通过状态干预与缓存干预分析失败原因；另设延迟回忆任务测试对过去表示的选择性检索。

Result: 在规则30元胞自动机上，BUT 对未见循环深度的外推比 CoTFormer 更可靠，但精度最终仍下降。干预实验表明 CoTFormer 的失败源于状态与缓存的相互作用：修正当前状态可暂时恢复精度，而保留的历史会削弱该修正。在延迟回忆任务中，BUT 同样优于 CoTFormer，后者不能可靠选中被请求的缓存表示。LSTM-UT 在深度外推与延迟回忆上均优于上述基线。

Conclusion: 有界门控记忆作为重复计算及后续检索的有效归纳偏置得到实验支持，优于仅保留当前状态或无界扩展缓存的方式。

Abstract: Recurrent-depth Transformers apply shared computation repeatedly, but differ in how they retain information across steps. We compare a Block Universal Transformer (BUT), which carries only its current hidden state; CoTFormer, which also retains an expanding attention cache; and a new LSTM Universal Transformer (LSTM-UT) with bounded gated memory. On Rule 30 cellular automata, BUT extrapolates to unseen recurrent depths more reliably than CoTFormer, although its accuracy eventually degrades. State and cache interventions show that CoTFormer's failure depends on their interaction: correcting the current state can temporarily restore accuracy, while retained history can undermine that correction. In a delayed-recall task, BUT also outperforms CoTFormer despite lacking direct access to past states; CoTFormer does not reliably select the requested cached representation. LSTM-UT improves both depth extrapolation and delayed recall over these baselines. The results support bounded gated memory as an effective inductive bias for repeated computation and later retrieval in these tasks.

</details>


### [226] [FedFIbOS: Fisher Importance based Optimal Submodelling for Heterogeneous Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19559)
*Yasmeen Afzal,Jeremiah D. Deng,Haibo Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 FedFIbOS，一种基于 Fisher 信息重要性原则的异构联邦学习子模型参数选择方法，从理论上推导出子模型选择准则（Fisher 加权二次掩蔽代理），并提供高效的经验对角 Fisher 估计，在多个非 IID 数据集上取得约 10% 的精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构联邦学习中不同计算能力的客户端只能训练子模型，现有方法多采用参数幅值等启发式重要性度量来选择子模型参数，但这些准则缺乏收敛理论支撑；同时部分客户端参与会在 Fisher 分数中引入额外的估计效应。论文旨在填补这一理论空白，为参数选择提供有理论依据的准则。

Method: 作者提出 FedFIbOS（Fisher Importance-based Optimal Submodelling），利用 Fisher 信息构建最小化子模型掩蔽误差的原则性准则。具体地，通过 Fisher 加权的二次掩蔽代理来形式化子模型选择，并证明在 Fisher-dominant 排序条件下，FedFIbOS 所采用的原始 Fisher top-k 规则能求解该代理。Fisher 分数由经验对角 Fisher 信息（利用平方梯度）高效估计，在稳定且自适应的参数选择的同时不引入额外优化开销。

Result: 在 CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100 和 AGNews 上的病理性非 IID 与 Dirichlet 非 IID 设置下，FedFIbOS 的精度比当前最优方法（SOTA）高出约 10%，且提升幅度随异质性增强而更加显著。

Conclusion: 论文为异构联邦学习的子模型参数选择提供了理论依据，证明 Fisher 信息重要性能有效替代缺乏理论支撑的启发式度量；FedFIbOS 保留了底层掩蔽联邦优化界的收敛结构，并通过高效 Fisher 估计实现稳定、自适应的选择，实验验证了其优越性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Heterogeneous federated learning requires clients with diverse computational capacities to collaboratively train a global model, where each client trains a capacity-constrained submodel. Existing methods select submodel parameters using heuristic importance measures---most prominently parameter magnitude---without theoretical justification for why these measures support convergence. We identify a fundamental gap: existing parameter selection criteria lack theoretical grounding in the convergence framework, partial client participation introduces additional estimation effects in the Fisher scores. We propose \textbf{FedFIbOS}: Fisher Importance-based Optimal Submodelling for heterogeneous federated learning, using Fisher Information in a principled criterion derived from minimizing submodel masking error. %We formally establish when magnitude selection is equivalent to Fisher selection fail under non-IID heterogeneous federated learning. We theoretically formulate submodel selection through a Fisher-weighted quadratic masking surrogate and show that the raw Fisher top-$k$ rule implemented by FedFIbOS solves this surrogate under a Fisher-dominant ranking condition. The resulting method retains the convergence structure of the underlying masked federated optimization bound. Fisher scores are efficiently estimated from empirical diagonal Fisher information using squared gradients, enabling stable and adaptive parameter selection without additional optimization overhead. Experiments on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and AGNews under pathological and Dirichlet non-IID settings show FedFIbOS achieves ${\approx}10\%$ higher accuracy than the state of the art, with improvements becoming more pronounced under stronger heterogeneity.

</details>


### [227] [The Complexity Kink: A Prompt-Side Structural Complexity Index for Code-Generation Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19616)
*Michael Hernandez,Tian Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个在代码生成前即可评分的六维提示词结构复杂度指数，并将其与正确性解耦。通过对5,000个Python提示词的多评分者信度检验（ICC=0.872）、21个模型共105,000次生成的观测分析，发现通过率随提示词复杂度变化存在非单调断点，但该断点受任务类型、构造框架和模型差异影响，并非通用阈值；最后强调该复合指数仅作测量工具，不作因果解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从生成代码反推的复杂度是"依赖失败"的：一个困难的提示可能产出很短的失败程序，从而被误判为低复杂度。为在代码生成发生之前独立刻画提示词难度，需要构建一个与正确性分离的提示词侧结构复杂度度量。

Method: 设计六维提示词侧结构复杂度指数，在生成前评分并与正确性分离；按初步单人评分量规的六个区间选取5,000个Python提示词；由4位面板外LLM评分者重新评分得到19,997行评分（ICC=0.872，基于全部4次评分的4,998个提示词）；对每个提示词评估21个模型，共105,000次生成。分析采用未调整的均值池化断点分析、任务类型固定效应、构造框架控制、模型特异拟合等方法；并用365提示词审计清洁扩展集、零通过生成的可计算Lizard复杂度、不一致性富集校准集、释义与跨语言重评分，以及2SLS过度识别检验进行稳健性与识别验证。

Result: 未调整均值池化分析中，通过率在复合分13.75处出现非单调断点（≤13.75通过率79.9%，以上87.6%），差值7.6个百分点；该断点不是通用失效阈值。加入任务类型固定效应后断点移至10.75，区间差缩小到2.1点；加入构造框架控制后断点移至8.50，原始差为-3.5点，单一框架无法复现+7.6点的池化变化。模型特异拟合有16个上升、5个下降。365提示词清洁扩展集在区间15和16与原五模型估计吻合，但区间16以上仅新增14个提示词。在可计算Lizard复杂度的零通过生成中，28.5%同时满足提示复合分>8且输出复杂度≤10。人类一致性中等且依赖评分者；释义与跨语言重评分保持评分排序。过度识别检验拒绝六维联合限制，故复合分仅作为指数，2SLS估计不作因果解释。

Conclusion: 本文贡献在于提出一个生成前的测量框架，并提供一个有界观测分析来刻画信度区间。提示词侧复杂度与通过率的关系存在断点但高度依赖任务类型、构造框架与模型，无法作为通用失效阈值；复合指数仅用于测量而非因果推断。

Abstract: Complexity measured from generated code is failure-dependent: a difficult prompt can yield a short failing program and be assigned low output complexity. We introduce a six-dimension prompt-side structural-complexity index scored before generation and kept separate from correctness. We select 5,000 Python prompts across six bands of a preliminary single-rater rubric. Four out-of-panel LLM raters rescore the locked prompts, giving 19,997 score rows; composite inter-rater reliability is ICC = 0.872 on the 4,998 prompts with all four ratings. We evaluate 21 models per prompt, yielding 105,000 generations. In the unadjusted mean-pooled analysis, pass rate has a nonmonotone breakpoint at composite 13.75, with 79.9% at or below and 87.6% above. This is not a universal failure cutoff. Task-type fixed effects shift the breakpoint to 10.75 and cut the regime gap from 7.6 to 2.1 points. A construction-frame control shifts it to 8.50 with a raw gap of -3.5 points, and neither frame alone reproduces the pooled +7.6-point change. Model-specific fits include 16 upward and five downward changes. A 365-prompt audit-clean extension matches the original five-model estimates at bins 15 and 16 but adds only 14 prompts above bin 16. Among zero-pass generations with computable Lizard complexity, 28.5% pair a prompt composite above 8 with output complexity at most 10. Human agreement is moderate and rater-dependent on a disagreement-enriched calibration set; paraphrase and cross-language rescoring preserve score ordering. Overidentification tests reject the joint restrictions on the six dimensions, so we treat the composite as an index and make no causal interpretation of the 2SLS estimates. The contribution is a pre-generation measurement framework and a bounded observational analysis of reliability regimes.

</details>


### [228] [A Policy Profile for Croissant: Refusal as a Property of the Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19640)
*Alexander Chernov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为 ML 数据集描述符 Croissant 补充了缺失的"使用条件求值"这一半：通过一个可加性 profile，使数据集能声明其允许的操作及条件，并给出五种运算符的完整判定过程，使门禁（gate）仅凭描述符即可决策并记录所检查内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Croissant 自 1.1 版起可携带数据使用条件（推荐 DUO 和 ODRL），但所有版本均未规定这些条件如何被求值：没有判定过程、没有求值成本上界、没有对实现无法求值的条件的处理结果、没有已检查内容的记录，也缺少与调用方权限的组合规则。本文旨在补齐这一"求值语义"空缺。

Method: 作者提出一个可加性 profile，让数据集在封闭的五运算符集合上声明其许可的操作及许可条件，并给出完整的判定过程；门禁仅依据描述符即可决策并记录检查内容。通过两个语料库评估：一个由真实 nf-core 流水线门控的三个描述符给出部署结果；另一个由 profile 语法生成、覆盖每个运算符、拒绝类别和一致性条款的语料库给出广度评估。

Result: 部署结果：profile 文档产生的决策与门禁原生描述符逐记录一致；剥离该层后仍是合法 Croissant 文档；相对于 119 μs 的决策，额外开销仅 11.7 μs。广度结果：552 个完整决策记录在原生描述符、profile 术语、以及 usageInfo 中作为 ODRL 的同一策略三方面一致。调用方限定与数据限定策略遍历不相交的状态空间，因此二者的许可集合互不包含。

Conclusion: 承载形式（carrier）并非贡献所在，求值语义才是关键贡献。该 profile 为 Croissant 的数据使用条件提供了明确、低成本、可审计的求值机制。

Abstract: Croissant is the de facto machine-readable descriptor for ML datasets: JSON-LD over schema.org. Since version 1.1 it also carries data use conditions, recommending DUO and ODRL for them. What no version specifies is how any of them is evaluated: no decision procedure, no bound on evaluation cost, no outcome for a condition an implementation cannot evaluate, no record of what was checked, and nothing on composition with caller-side authority. We supply that half. An additive profile lets a dataset declare the operations it admits and the conditions under which it admits them, over a closed set of five operators whose decision procedure is given in full, so a gate decides from the descriptor alone and records what it checked. Two corpora evaluate it and their evidence is kept apart. Three descriptors that gated a real nf-core pipeline give the deployment result: decisions from a profile document match the gate's native descriptor record for record, stripping the layer leaves a valid Croissant document, and the added cost is 11.7 $μ$s against a 119 $μ$s decision. A corpus generated from the profile's grammar gives the breadth, covering every operator, refusal class and conformance clause. Across its valid cases, 552 complete decision records agree three ways -- native descriptor, profile terms, and the same policy as ODRL in usageInfo. The carrier is therefore not the contribution; the evaluation semantics is. Finally, caller-bound and data-bound policies range over non-overlapping state spaces, so neither permit set contains the other.

</details>


### [229] [CoRe: Coherence and Relational Alignment for Multivariate Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19670)
*Xiaoyu Lin,Huiran Duan,Yining Liu,Zhixiang Wu,Chu Lin,Lin Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 CoRe，一种无需额外可训练参数、仅通过更换损失函数即可应用的模型无关学习目标，用于提升多元时序直接预测中未来轨迹的结构一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接预测已成为多元时序预测的标准范式，但其训练目标往往仍被分解为 MSE 等逐点误差。这类目标虽提供稳定监督，却未显式保持未来轨迹的结构——包括各变量内部的时间相干性和跨变量的关系一致性——从而导致预测结构被削弱。

Method: 提出 CoRe 学习目标，用两个输出空间约束替代逐点监督：一是频率相干损失，对齐预测序列与目标序列的频谱；二是低秩关系图损失，在目标派生的 PCA 子空间中匹配采样得到的成对差分。该目标不引入任何可训练参数，仅需修改损失即可应用于现有预测骨干网络。

Result: 在标准基准上的实验表明，CoRe 能提升强基线模型，与近期提出的预测目标相比表现更有竞争力，并在不同骨干网络、数据集和超参数设置下总体上保持持续有效。

Conclusion: CoRe 是一种轻量、模型无关且通用的学习目标，通过在输出空间施加频谱相干性和低秩关系一致性约束，有效改善多元时序直接预测的结构保持能力。

Abstract: Direct forecasting has become a standard paradigm for multivariate time-series forecasting because it predicts the full future horizon in a single pass. However, its training objective is often still decomposed into pointwise errors such as MSE. Such objectives provide stable supervision, but they do not explicitly preserve the structure of the future trajectory: temporal coherence within each variable and relational consistency across variables can both be weakened. We propose CoRe, a model-agnostic learning objective for direct multivariate forecasting. CoRe replaces pointwise supervision with two output-space constraints: a frequency coherence loss that aligns predicted and target spectra, and a low-rank relational graph loss that matches sampled pairwise differences in a target-derived PCA subspace. The resulting objective introduces no trainable parameters and can be applied to existing forecasting backbones by changing only the loss. Experiments on standard benchmarks show that CoRe improves strong baselines, compares favorably with recent forecasting objectives, and remains effective across different backbones, datasets, and hyperparameter settings overall consistently.

</details>


### [230] [Conservation Buys Stability and Factoring Buys Counterfactuals in Physical World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19674)
*Yufeng Wang,Parivesh Priye,Lu Wei,Haibin Ling*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 学习模拟器在训练条件改变时会出现两类独立失效：长时程漂移与干预下无法遵循新物理规律。本文证明这两类失效需不同的结构补救——辛积分器+学习能量的演化保障长时程几何稳定性，显式线性因子化耦合编码保障反事实迁移，且二者可分离（双分离）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习到的模拟器能准确复现训练条件，但条件一旦改变会出现两种失败：长时间 rollout 中误差累积导致轨迹偏离物理合理行为；对物理参数施加干预时，模型仍遵循训练时学到的规律而非干预后的新规律。需要理解这两类失败是否源于相同或不同的结构缺陷，从而给出可用设计原则。

Method: 以三体系统为核心基准，设计匹配对照实验逐一移除或替换单个结构组件：(1) 用辛积分器演化学习到的能量以保持保守动力学的几何结构，对比等容量的普通预测器、能量正则化预测器和调优的神经ODE；(2) 通过显式线性因子化编码物理耦合，对比无约束参数化；并从像素推断状态而非直接给定状态验证结论。通过"双分离"实验：移除长时程稳定性结构而保留反事实迁移，以及移除因子化耦合而保留稳定性，来证明两机制彼此独立。

Result: 辛积分器+学习能量的模型在训练时长的100倍范围内仍保持有界且物理合理的轨迹，而等容量预测器、能量正则化预测器和调优神经ODE均发散。显式线性因子化使模型能遵循从未见过的耦合符号，而无约束参数化则锁定于训练规律。双分离实验显示：移除长时程稳定性结构不损害反事实迁移，移除因子化耦合则摧毁反事实迁移却不影响稳定性。该结论在三体系统之外以及仅从像素推断状态时依然成立。

Conclusion: 长时程稳定性与改变规律下的泛化源于不同的结构性承诺，二者可分别有意施加而不依赖彼此，为物理世界模型提供了具体的设计原则。

Abstract: A learned simulator can reproduce its training conditions accurately yet fail in two distinct ways once those conditions change. Over long rollouts, small errors accumulate until the trajectory drifts away from physically plausible behavior; under an intervention on a physical parameter, the model may continue to follow the law seen during training rather than the intervened one. We show that these two failures require different structural remedies. Evolving a learned energy with a symplectic integrator preserves the geometry of the conservative dynamics and keeps rollouts bounded and physically meaningful for up to $100\times$ the training horizon, while equal-capacity predictors, an energy-regularized predictor, and a tuned neural ODE diverge. By contrast, encoding the physical coupling through an explicit linear factorization enables the model to follow a never-seen sign of that coupling, whereas an unrestricted parameterization remains locked to the training law. Crucially, the two mechanisms are separable: removing the structure responsible for long-horizon stability leaves counterfactual transfer intact, while removing the factorized coupling destroys counterfactual transfer without eliminating stability. This double dissociation, established with matched controls that remove or replace one structural component at a time, persists beyond the headline three-body system and remains visible when the physical state must be inferred from pixels rather than provided directly. The result is a concrete design principle for physical world models: long-horizon stability and changed-law generalization arise from distinct structural commitments, and each can be imposed deliberately without requiring the other.

</details>


### [231] [Opinion Dynamics-based Coalition Formation for Federated Learning in Heterogeneous IoT Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19695)
*Mohammed El Hanjri,Anas Abouaomar,Hamidou Tembine,Abdellatif Kobbane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于Hegselmann-Krause有界置信意见动力学的联邦学习客户端联盟形成方法，在局部权重空间中按相似度聚合客户端，用于智能水表短期用水量预测，显著降低MAE并提升全局准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在异构物联网场景（如智慧城市水表计量网络）中，统计异质性导致FedAvg将差异较大的本地模型简单平均为一个全局模型，无法捕捉客户端特有的模式，影响预测性能。

Method: 在局部权重空间中直接形成客户端联盟，并将联盟形成建模为作用于局部权重的Hegselmann-Krause有界置信意见动力学过程；开发了基于欧氏距离和余弦相似度两种置信准则的HK交互变体，在联盟层级进行聚合。将该框架应用于使用本地LSTM模型的短期用水量预测。

Result: 在真实智能水表用水数据集上，所提HK联盟形成方法在最多十次内部迭代内形成稳定的内生联盟结构；与FedAvg相比不增加客户端计算和通信开销；平均MAE相对FedAvg降低最高54%、相对FedProx降低39%、相对Per-FedAvg降低24%，并取得最高全局准确率（83-85%）。

Conclusion: 基于HK有界置信的权重空间联盟形成方法能有效应对联邦学习中的统计异质性，在用水量预测任务上以零额外计算通信代价显著优于FedAvg、FedProx、Per-FedAvg等基线，表明客户端联盟聚合是提升异构联邦学习性能的有效途径。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables privacy-preserving, on-device training across heterogeneous Internet-of-Things (IoT) deployments such as smart-city water-metering networks, where each smart meter observes a household-specific consumption time series. Under such statistical heterogeneity, the standard Federated Averaging (FedAvg) aggregation averages dissimilar local models into a single global model that may fail to capture client-specific patterns. We address this by forming client coalitions directly in the local-weight space and aggregating at the coalition level. Extending a prior weight-driven coalition-formation scheme, we model coalition formation as a Hegselmann-Krause (HK) bounded-confidence opinion-dynamics process acting on the local weights, and develop variants of the HK interaction based on Euclidean-distance and cosine-similarity confidence criteria. The framework is applied to short-term water-consumption forecasting with local Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) models and evaluated against FedAvg, Per-FedAvg, FedProx, and FedAvg with Euclidean-distance or cosine-similarity coalition formation. Experiments on a real smart-metering dataset of water consumption show that the proposed HK-based coalition formation produces stable, endogenous coalition structures within at most ten inner iterations, incurs no additional client-side computation or communication compared to FedAvg, and reduces the average MAE by up to 54% relative to FedAvg, 39% relative to FedProx, and 24% relative to Per-FedAvg, while achieving the highest global accuracy (83-85%).

</details>


### [232] [Odds-Ratio Thompson Sampling: A Specification and Design Guide for Contrast-Based Multi-Armed Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19709)
*Sulgi Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Odds-Ratio Thompson Sampling（OR-TS），一种面向分批更新多臂老虎机的算法，它携带对数几率对比（log-odds contrasts）的联合后验，并在每批中重新拟合并边缘化公共水平，以应对批次间共享水平漂移导致的绝对奖励率记忆过时问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分批多臂老虎机通常在各臂之间传递绝对奖励率记忆，但当批次间共享水平发生漂移时，该绝对记忆会过时，尽管臂与臂之间的对比可能并未改变。作者希望设计一种只记忆相对对比、每批重新拟合公共水平的更新方式，以避免水平漂移带来的偏差。

Method: 作者提出 OR-TS：在贝叶斯 bandit agent 内对 log-odds 对比的联合后验进行更新，并在每批中重新拟合并边缘化公共水平。算法带有两个控制参数：decay（控制过去证据在更新后的存留程度）和 aggressiveness（控制信念转化为分配的锐度）。将其与绝对率记忆算法进行对比评估。

Result: 在 86 个公开 A/B 序列中，水平的变化约为对比的 25 倍。在预先设定的合成环境中，移动水平使绝对率记忆的 regret 增加五倍，且在 20 次运行中有 7 次最优臂获得多数流量的比例不足，而 OR-TS 为零。在基于 71 个真实实验构建的策略模拟中（对比过小而无法分辨），58 个实验的期望点击差异保持在 0.1% 以内，但对比记忆最终落在较优臂上的频率仍高出两倍以上。在对比本身也移动的场景下，该策略失败，文中亦报告了此情形。

Conclusion: OR-TS 通过记忆对数几率对比而非绝对奖励率，能有效缓解共享水平漂移带来的记忆过时问题，在水平和对比的移动场景下表现优于绝对率记忆，但其有效性依赖于对比本身相对稳定的假设，当对比也移动时该方法失效。

Abstract: Batched multi-armed bandits update on a service's own schedule, and the usual implementation carries each arm's absolute reward rate from one update to the next. When the shared level moves between batches, that memory goes stale even though the comparisons between arms may not have. Odds-Ratio Thompson Sampling (OR-TS) instead carries the joint posterior over log-odds contrasts and fits the common level afresh in every batch, marginalizing it out. This paper specifies that update, places it inside a Bayesian bandit agent with two controls, decay for how much past evidence survives an update and aggressiveness for how sharply belief becomes allocation, and evaluates it against absolute-rate memory. Across 86 public A/B series the level varies about twenty-five times more than the contrast. In prespecified synthetic environments a moving level costs absolute-rate memory five times the regret and leaves the best arm below a majority of traffic in 7 of 20 runs, against none for OR-TS. In a policy simulation built from 71 real experiments, where the contrasts are too small to resolve, expected-click differences stay within 0.1% for 58 of them, yet contrast memory still ends on the better arm more than twice as often. Where the contrasts themselves move, the bet fails, and that case is reported too.

</details>


### [233] [Alliance Beats Isolation: Unifying Heterogeneous Allied Datasets Improves Classifier Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19748)
*Girish Keshav Palshikar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种方法与流程，将一对特征空间异构但类别标签相同、对象不相交的“关联数据集”(allied datasets) 合并到统一特征空间，并用矩阵补全构建统一数据集，从而让单个分类器可跨数据集迁移知识并显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多应用领域（如学生辍学、保险欺诈、贷款审批、机器故障）存在大量公开的关联数据集：对象集合互不相交、类别标签相同，但特征空间大部分异构、仅有少量共享特征。由于特征空间不一致，无法在同一数据集上联合训练单个分类器，也无法将一个数据集上训练的模型直接用于另一个数据集，导致各数据集上的可用信息未被充分利用。

Method: 提出一种将一对关联异构数据集的特征空间合并为单一特征空间的方法；随后利用矩阵补全(matrix completion)方法，基于合并后的特征空间构造统一数据集。其核心假设是：合并后的表示有助于分类知识从一个数据集迁移到另一个数据集。

Result: 在多对关联异构数据集及多种分类器上的实验表明，在统一表示上训练的任何分类器，其性能始终优于仅在各关联数据集上分别训练的分类器。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种简便途径，通过统一并联合使用多个关联数据集，可显著提升分类器性能，验证了合并表示在异构数据集间迁移分类知识的有效性。

Abstract: In many application domains, such as student dropout, insurance fraud, loan approval, and machine failures, several labelled public datasets are available where (i) data is about the same type of objects but the set of actual underlying objects are disjoint; and (ii) the class labels are same; and (iii) the feature spaces of the datasets are largely distinct (heterogeneous), with a few shared features. We call such datasets as allied. A single classifier cannot be trained on both datasets together, and one classifier trained on one dataset cannot be tested on the other. In this paper, we propose a method to merge the feature-spaces into a single feature-space for a pair of given allied heterogeneous datasets. We then use a matrix completion method to create a unified dataset based on the merged feature-space. The hypothesis is that the merged representation facilitates the transfer of classification knowledge from one dataset to another. We conduct experiments on several pairs of allied, heterogeneous datasets and several classifiers to demonstrate that any classifier trained on the unified representation always outperforms classifiers separately trained on the constituent allied datasets on several pairs of allied datasets. This work provides an easy way to substantially improve classifier performance by unifying and using multiple allied datasets together.

</details>


### [234] [OceanMoE: Structured Conditional Sparse Computation for Long-Horizon Multivariate Ocean Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19768)
*Yishun Zhu,Jian Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 OceanMoE，一种结构化条件稀疏混合专家框架，在统一模型中实现多变量海洋预报的共享与专业化，并在 ORAS5 长时程自回归预报上降低了误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量海洋预报需要利用耦合海洋系统中的共享演化规律，同时又要适配不同预报变量与空间位置在统计和动力学特征上的异质性。全共享模型缺乏处理异质性的灵活性，全独立模型则丢弃了跨变量共享的海洋背景，因此需要一种既保留共享上下文又能按预报目标和局部状态进行专业化计算的统一建模方案。

Method: 提出 OceanMoE，一个结构化条件稀疏 Mixture-of-Experts 框架。它融合跨变量信息构建目标特定的局部表示，并据此在每一空间位置进行内容条件化的稀疏路由，活跃专家数量根据路由器置信度自适应调整。解码器中，路由额外引入由球谐空间基函数参数化的可学习地理偏置；同时共享的残差通路和季节通路提供共同的跨变量上下文与月份相关背景。

Result: 在 ORAS5 长时程自回归预报实验中，OceanMoE 在两种评测设定下均降低了整体预报误差，并在大多数后续 rollout 月份保持比相应基线更低的几何平均归一化 RMSE。路由分析显示专家分配随预报目标和空间位置而变化。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，结构化条件计算是一种有效的建模策略，能够在共享海洋背景与自适应专业化之间取得平衡，为多变量海洋预报提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Multivariate ocean forecasting must exploit shared evolution in a coupled ocean system while adapting to the heterogeneous statistical and dynamical characteristics of different prediction variables and locations. Fully shared models may lack the flexibility to handle this heterogeneity, whereas fully independent models discard the common ocean context shared across variables. The key question is how to retain shared context in a unified model while allowing computation to specialize according to the prediction target and local state. We propose OceanMoE, a structured conditional sparse Mixture-of-Experts framework that combines sharing and specialization for multivariate ocean forecasting. OceanMoE fuses cross-variable information to construct target-specific local representations and uses them to perform content-conditioned sparse routing at each spatial location, with the number of active experts adapted to router confidence. In the decoder, routing is augmented with a learned geographic bias parameterized by spherical-harmonic spatial bases, while shared residual and seasonal pathways provide common cross-variable and month-dependent context. Experiments on long-horizon autoregressive ORAS5 forecasting show that OceanMoE lowers aggregate forecasting error in both evaluated settings and maintains lower geometric-mean normalized RMSE than the corresponding baselines over most later rollout months. Routing analyses further show that expert allocation varies with prediction targets and spatial locations. These results support structured conditional computation as a modeling strategy for balancing shared ocean context with adaptive specialization.

</details>


### [235] [PhyRestore: Physics-Structured Latent-Factor Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19776)
*Ahmed Shafee,Chayan Lahiri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 PhyRestore，一种物理结构化的潜因子恢复框架，用于在物理因子受噪声或损坏时估计土壤流失的时间变化，并在流域尺度双时相栅格数据上与多种模型进行了对比评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在输入物理因子存在噪声或损坏时，估计土壤流失的时间变化十分困难，尤其是实质性变化相对于大量几乎不变的位置而言极为稀少，导致直接预测或修正退化估计都难以可靠捕捉高量级变化。

Method: 基于修正通用土壤流失方程(RUSLE)，引入 PhyRestore 框架：不直接预测土壤流失变化或修正退化的物理估计，而是恢复受损的物理因子（如降雨侵蚀力和覆盖管理因子），再通过已知的物理关系重构时间变化。并在流域尺度双时相栅格设定下，对降雨侵蚀力与覆盖管理因子进行单独及同时破坏，与退化 RUSLE 估计及 Direct RF、XGBoost、MLP、CNN 模型对比。

Result: 当受损因子仍可辨识时，因子恢复能提升高量级变化的复原效果；但在联合损坏、正极端值稀疏、以及因子值超出训练支撑范围时，其优势会减弱。

Conclusion: PhyRestore 通过恢复物理因子并复用已知物理关系，在因子可辨识条件下优于直接预测与退化估计方法，但其性能受限于联合损坏、极端值稀疏性和训练数据覆盖范围。

Abstract: Estimating temporal soil-loss change is challenging when physically meaningful input factors are noisy or corrupted, particularly because substantial changes are rare relative to the large number of locations exhibiting little change. We study this problem through the Revised Universal Soil Loss Equation (RUSLE) and introduce PhyRestore, a physics-structured latent-factor restoration framework. Rather than directly predicting soil-loss change or correcting a degraded physical estimate, PhyRestore restores corrupted physical factors and reconstructs temporal change through the known physical relationship. We evaluate PhyRestore in a watershed-scale bitemporal raster setting under isolated and simultaneous corruption of rainfall erosivity and cover management, comparing it with the degraded RUSLE estimate and Direct RF, XGBoost, MLP, and CNN models. Factor restoration improves high-magnitude recovery when the corrupted factors remain identifiable, but its advantage weakens under joint corruption, sparse positive extremes, and factor values outside the training support.

</details>


### [236] [Learning-Based Reconstruction of Optical Properties in Bilayered Media from Single-distance Time-Resolved Reflectance Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19786)
*Caterina Amendola,Giulia Maffeis,Lorenzo Buffoni,Lorenzo Chicchi,Francesco Coghi,Duccio Fanelli,Raffaele Marino,Fabrizio Martelli,Riccardo Paoli,Lorenzo Pattelli,Lorenzo Spinelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种机器学习框架，用于从时间分辨反射测量（DTOF）中重建双层生物介质的光学特性（吸收与散射系数），并与基于扩散方程的模型算法基准对比，在精度和速度上均取得更优表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于扩散方程的逆向求解器在处理层状生物介质时面临结构性非均质性困难，对浅层吸收和深层散射的重建精度较差。因此需要一种能克服扩散理论固有近似、且不依赖层数先验信息的替代方案。

Method: 使用精确的蒙特卡洛仿真在多个源-探测器距离下生成稳健的前向DTOF合成数据集；在该数据集上训练机器学习流水线，并与最先进的基于模型的逆向重建方法进行验证与对比。未来可扩展为联合多距离重建，以进一步提升精度。

Result: 相比基于模型的逆向求解器，机器学习方法在重建速度上显著加快、精度更高，且无需关于介质层数的任何先验信息即可估计参数空间维度。

Conclusion: 机器学习框架有效解决了双层介质光学性质重建中扩散理论近似带来的精度问题，兼具高效与准确性。通过联合多距离DTOF曲线训练可望在未来进一步改善重建精度。

Abstract: The inverse problem of reconstructing optical properties, specifically absorption and scattering coefficients, in layered biological media from time-domain reflectance measurements remains a significant challenge for traditional analytical models. Inverse solvers based on the diffusion equation often struggle with structural heterogeneity, frequently yielding poor accuracy for superficial absorption and deep-layers scattering. In this work, we propose a machine learning framework as an alternative approach to reconstruct the optical properties of a bilayered medium, benchmarking its efficiency and accuracy against model-based algorithms. To overcome the intrinsic approximations of diffusion theory and inverse reconstruction, we generated a robust synthetic dataset of forward DTOF using exact Monte Carlo simulations at multiple source-detector distances. A machine learning pipeline was then trained on this dataset and validated against state-of-the-art model-based reconstruction methods. Besides the significant reconstruction speed-up, the machine learning approach achieves higher accuracy than model-based inverse solvers, further providing an estimate of the parameter space dimensionality without requiring any a priori information about the number of layers in the investigated geometry. Further enhancements in the reconstruction accuracy can be expected in future extensions of this work, by training the pipeline over multiple DTOF curves from the same medium, in a joint multi-distance reconstruction approach.

</details>


### [237] [DeliveryGym: An RL Environment for Long-Horizon Embodied Agent Planning with Adaptive Curriculum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19801)
*Haoqiang Kang,Yiming Zhang,Yiyang Guo,Chuying Li,Jianzhi Shen,Tianruo Rose Xu,Xiaokang Ye,Lianhui Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DeliveryGym 是一个用于训练与评估 LLM 智能体执行连续性快递轮班的 3D 可执行环境，通过持久世界动力学与全程轨迹奖励，让智能体在学习执行任务的同时学会为后续订单节省资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可执行环境往往只关注当前任务是否成功，忽视了完成一项任务（如派送）会消耗时间、能量或金钱，从而影响后续工作。作者认为要让智能体真正学会规划，环境必须保留这些跨任务依赖，并将它们转化为贯穿完整轨迹的反馈信号。

Method: 提出 DeliveryGym，将多模态工具交互与持久世界动力学耦合，并从模拟器事件计算轨迹奖励，使决策成本可用于强化学习（RL）。环境还会根据策略的已观测弱点自适应地调整未来的训练轮班，同时保持评估集固定不变。

Result: 在 6 个模型和 13 张城市地图上，评估显示“可靠执行已分配派送”与“在轮班中自主选择和排序工作”之间存在能力差距。在固定测试集上，RL 使 Qwen3-VL-4B 的净收入提升 54.3%；在相同 rollout 预算下，自适应训练环境相比均匀采样使测试收入再提升 16.5%。

Conclusion: DeliveryGym 提供了一个可执行环境，用于研究智能体如何在一个回合内协调派送任务并为后续订单保留资源，证明从完整轮班中学习以及训练情境的自适应选择都对性能有显著增益。

Abstract: Executable environments enable LLM agents to learn from the consequences of their actions.
